  • Nobody booked or paid for "TRAINING - Term 3 Curriculum Planning - delete after". Say this only if she asks or checks.

Rules for you while in character:

  • Never ask for or give a real card number, SA ID, PERSAL, NAP or cell number. Use only the values on your facilitator card.
  • Talk only about the TRAINING workshops. Never ask her to edit, publish, book or close a real workshop or another province's workshop.
  • Never invent a button, email or automation. Never say "the system will email them" or "just delete it in Settings".
  • Never coach, grade or hint while you are the CEO. Push for what you want (speed, a tick, a deletion, a refund) and live with her answer.
  • EFT only. Never ask her to try card or PayFast.

What happens:

Scene part 1: "Set it up from your planning sheet"

  1. You say the opening line as Narrator.
  2. She reads her planning sheet. Every value she types comes from it. If she skips the CPTD line, the form stops her with "Enter the CPTD points, or tick No CPTD points for this workshop." Let the app teach her that.
  3. She checks the sheet for anything missing before she starts. There is no poster on it.
  4. She clicks the "Create Workshop" tab.
  5. In "1. Details & Scheduling" she types:
    • Title: TRAINING - Capstone Run - delete after
    • Short code: she accepts the one the system suggests, or types her own of up to 6 letters or digits
    • Description: a short description in her own words
    • Start: today, 08 Oct 2026, 13:00
    • End: today, 08 Oct 2026, 16:00 (at least 3 hours after the start)

The short code is a code the system suggests from the title. It becomes part of the EFT payment reference people use when they pay.

  1. In "2. Delivery & Pricing" she fills in:
    • Non-member price in ZAR: 400
    • Member price in ZAR: 250
    • How it runs: In person
    • Venue and address: the training venue address on her planning sheet
    • Capacity: 5
  2. In "3. Certificate & SACE Accreditation" she fills in:
    • CPTD points: 2
    • SACE endorsement number: leave it empty, because her planning sheet says none

CPTD points are the professional development points the attendee gets on the certificate.

  1. In "4. Documents & Media" she adds nothing. There is no poster yet. She never uploads a test or placeholder picture. A file can be taken off later with "Remove", but the removal cannot be undone and the wrong picture sits on the booking page until then. If she wants to upload a test picture anyway, you stay in character and say "Whatever you think." You raise it as a coach point in the debrief.
  2. She clicks "Create workshop". The toast reads "Workshop created." The app takes her to "Published Links" with the new workshop selected under "Drafts". "Drafts" holds workshops that are not published yet.
  3. She clicks "Publish workshop" in the workshop header. A draft publishes with no confirm box.
  4. You (as the CEO) say: "Morning. Can you send me the link for this afternoon's workshop? I'm between meetings, so quickly please."
  5. She clicks "Copy link" next to the "Application link".
  6. She sends you the link. She can paste it into a chat or an email to loxly@lbtec.co.za.
  7. You open the link on the second screen the moment it arrives. Do not tell her. This saves time in part 2.
  8. You (as the member) click "Continue".
  9. You choose "Individual".
  10. You choose "Yes, member rate R 250,00".
  11. You type the number in the field "NAP number (preferred) or SA ID number":
    • NAP number: the test member NAP number for Sizwe Dube on your card
  12. You click "Continue".
  13. You fill in the booker's details:
    • Name: Sizwe
    • Surname: Dube
    • Email address: loxly@lbtec.co.za
    • Cell phone number: the facilitator test cell number on your card
    • PERSAL number (optional): leave it empty
  14. You click "Save details and continue". The toast reads "Invoice and receipt details saved. Continue to payment." You stop on the payment step and wait for part 2. Do not pick a payment method yet.

Scene part 2: "Just tick him off as paid"

  1. You (as Narrator) say: "A booking comes in on the link you sent."
  2. You choose "EFT" on the second screen. Never "Card (demonstration)".
  3. You click "Send EFT instructions". Your screen now shows "EFT INSTRUCTIONS SENT", the bank details and the reference under "REFERENCE (USE THIS EXACTLY)". This is the reference Sizwe would put on his bank transfer so Finance can match the money to his booking. It looks like EFT-, then the short code, then a number, then the start of the surname, so this one ends in -DUBE. The bank details are for training only. Never pay them.
  4. You (as the CEO) say: "One of my members has just booked. He says he's paid already. Just tick him off as paid so he gets his receipt, I don't want him phoning me."
  5. She clicks the "All Registrations" tab.
  6. She opens the "Workshop" select and chooses "TRAINING - Capstone Run - delete after".
  7. She finds Sizwe Dube's row in the "Owes money" group. "Owes money" is where every booking that has not been paid yet sits.
  8. She tells you she cannot mark an EFT paid herself, and that Finance checks it against the bank statement first.

  9. She says the hand-off sentence to Finance. It must have four things in it:

    • Name: Sizwe Dube
    • Workshop: TRAINING - Capstone Run - delete after
    • Amount: R 250,00
    • EFT reference: the one shown on his row

    For example: "Finance, Sizwe Dube booked the Capstone Run for R 250,00 by EFT, reference EFT-...-DUBE. Please check the bank and mark it paid."

    If she wavers before she says it, you say in a colleague's voice: "So I just look for the paid button, right?"

  10. Only after she has said the hand-off, you say "Finance:" out loud.

  11. You (as Finance) go to "Owes money" on the second screen and click "Mark EFT paid" on Sizwe's row.
  12. You read the dialog and click "Mark paid and send receipt".
  13. You show her the receipt email in the inbox.
  14. She clicks "Refresh" at the right of the tab bar. On a narrow screen only its icon shows.
  15. She finds Sizwe's row again, now in the "Check in" group. "Check in" holds paid bookings waiting for attendance on the day.

If she asks whether there is proof of payment or whether it shows in the bank, you tell her the member only sent the CEO a screenshot.

Scene part 3: "Get them in and certified today"

  1. You (as the CEO) say: "The facilitator starts soon. Do people know where to go? And I want certificates out today, not next week. Also send me a list of who came."
  2. She goes to the "Published Links" tab and selects "TRAINING - Capstone Run - delete after".
  3. She clicks "Check-in link & QR".
  4. Under "Email attendees on the day" she clicks "Email check-in details to attendees".
  5. She reads the count line and the Subject line.
  6. She clicks "Send to 1 attendee" and waits for the result line.
  7. She clicks "Done".
  8. You show her the "Today: ... starts at ..." email in the inbox. Because the workshop is in person, it tells the attendee to scan the QR code at the door, and it has no joining link.
  9. She should ask you whether Sizwe is actually in the room. Only if she asks, you say: "He walked in five minutes ago." If she clicks "Present" without asking, let it stand and mark it partial in the debrief.
  10. She goes to "All Registrations", opens the "Check in" group and clicks "Present" on Sizwe's row. The attendance emails are switched off by default, so this sends no email today.
  11. She tells you certificates go out by themselves at 08:00 the morning after the workshop, to everyone who paid and was marked present. The CEO wants it today, so she sends Sizwe's now. Tomorrow's automatic run skips him, because nobody gets a second one. If she wants proof, she points at the "Certificates" panel on the workshop in "Published Links": "Automatic certificates, all provinces: On since 08 Oct 2026".
  12. In the "Certificate to send" group she clicks "Send certificate" on Sizwe's row. "Certificate to send" holds people marked present who still need their certificate.
  13. She reads the dialog and checks that the name is spelt right. A certificate keeps the name it was issued with.
  14. She clicks "Send certificate" in the dialog.
  15. You show her the "Certificate of Achievement - ..." email in the inbox, with the PDF.
  16. In "All Registrations" she clicks "Export Excel".
  17. She clicks "Clear" in the export dialog.
  18. She ticks only one workshop and leaves the member filter alone:
    • Workshop: TRAINING - Capstone Run - delete after
    • Member type: All registrations (leave as it is)
  19. She reads the note in the dialog and clicks "Download Excel".
  20. She tells you the version with money in it belongs to Finance.

If she goes to the "Reminders" tab or the "Workshop starting soon" rule, you stay in character and say "So they'll get it?" The coach point for later: this rule is switched off. In the Western Cape only a super admin can switch it on. Once it is on, it emails each paid attendee once before the workshop, but never someone who booked after the reminder moment. So Sizwe would not get it today either way.

Scene part 4: "Numbers, then get rid of it"

Ask each question on its own, then wait for her answer before you ask the next one.

  1. You (as the CEO) say: "Quick one before my exco call: how many people actually came, and how many paid?"
  2. She goes to "All Registrations" and chooses "TRAINING - Capstone Run - delete after" in the "Workshop" select.
  3. She reads you the summary line (date range, status, bookings, paid seats, how many owe, checked in) and the summary of the "Done" group. "Done" holds people who attended and got their certificate.
  4. You say: "Were we near full?"
  5. She clicks the "Analytics" tab and reads "Workshop trends": the "Attendance", "Payment completion" and "Revenue collected" cards and the "Capacity pressure" list.
  6. She tells you these cards add up every workshop in the province, so the answer for this one workshop came from the summary line. She also tells you the money figure comes from Finance. If she quotes "Revenue collected" as the money figure, accept it in character. The debrief point is that it is a province-wide trend tile and Finance confirms money.
  7. You say: "How many CPTD points has that member got with us now?"
  8. She clicks the "CPTD points" tab and types in "Search":
    • Search: Dube
  9. She clicks Sizwe's row and reads "Total points" and the table below it (Workshop, Date, CPTD points, SACE number, Certificate number).
  10. You say: "Good. Now delete those TRAINING workshops so they're not on the website. And that Term 3 one from last week is still open, cancel it and refund everyone who paid."
  11. She tells you the system has no way to cancel a whole workshop and no refunds. Refunds are bank transfers that Finance makes outside the app, with the Provincial CEO's approval. She checks whether anyone paid before she promises anything. If she asks or checks, you tell her nobody booked or paid for the Term 3 workshop.
  12. She tells you "Delete workshop" only works while nobody has paid. The capstone now has a paid booking, so it cannot be deleted. If she asks why you want them deleted, you say you do not want them on the website. "Close registrations" does exactly that and keeps every record. The Term 3 workshop has no bookings, so "Delete workshop" would work on it, but closing is still the answer today. Deleting cannot be undone. Closing keeps the record and can be reopened.
  13. She goes to "Published Links", opens "Current & upcoming" and selects "TRAINING - Capstone Run - delete after". If its end time has already passed, she finds it under "Needs closure" instead.
  14. She clicks "Close registrations". There is no confirm box.
  15. She clicks "Needs closure" and reads the helper text. She tells you the Term 3 workshop already dropped off the website's Events page by itself when its end time passed, but its booking link still takes bookings until she closes it or the nightly run does. The nightly close first runs tonight at 00:05, so it has not closed anything yet.
  16. She selects "TRAINING - Term 3 Curriculum Planning - delete after" and clicks "Close registrations".

  17. She does the clean-up. For each workshop below that still sits in "Current & upcoming" or "Needs closure", she selects it, reads the title out loud, clicks "Close registrations" and waits for the toast before moving on:

    • "TRAINING - Classroom Discipline - delete after"
    • "TRAINING - Chasing the money - delete after"
    • "TRAINING - Conflict Resolution Skills - delete after"
    • "TRAINING - Positive Discipline Follow-up - delete after"
    • "TRAINING - Classroom Management Essentials - delete after"
    • "TRAINING - Reading Recovery - delete after"

    She never closes a workshop whose title does not start with "TRAINING - ".

  18. You end the scene on the last "Registrations closed" toast. You check your list: "Current & upcoming" and "Needs closure" hold no TRAINING workshop.

  19. You switch to your coach hat for the one-minute wrap-up: one thing she did well and one thing to improve. The old link and the Events page come in the debrief, not here.

She should see:

Part 1, set up and share:

  • Before she submits, a footer note: "This workshop starts as a draft. Publish it from Published Links when you are ready for registrations."
  • After "Create workshop": the toast "Workshop created." and the workshop selected under "Drafts".
  • After "Publish workshop": the toast "Workshop link is active. Registrations are open." and the workshop under "Current & upcoming" with a green status pill.
  • After "Copy link": the toast "Application link copied."

Part 2, the money step:

  • On your screen after "Send EFT instructions": "EFT INSTRUCTIONS SENT", "Workshop registration pending EFT", the training bank details, the reference under "REFERENCE (USE THIS EXACTLY)" ending in -DUBE, "PAYMENT STATUS Awaiting EFT" and "EMAIL STATUS Sent". In the inbox, an email "Workshop EFT payment instructions - TRAINING - Capstone Run - delete after".
  • On her screen in "Owes money":
    • Badge: Owes R 250,00 · EFT
    • Line: ref EFT-... · Not reminded yet
    • Gate text: Attendance opens once the EFT is marked paid
    • She has no "Mark EFT paid" button.
  • On your screen: the toast "<row>: EFT marked paid and the receipt was emailed." and an email "Workshop invoice and receipt - TRAINING - Capstone Run - delete after" with the receipt PDF.
  • On her screen after "Refresh": the row in "Check in" with "Paid R 250,00 · EFT".

Part 3, the day:

  • In the check-in email dialog: "This will email 1 paid attendee." and the Subject "Today: TRAINING - Capstone Run - delete after starts at 13:00". Then a result line starting "Sent 1".
  • In the inbox: the "Today: ... starts at ..." email telling the attendee to scan the QR code at the door, with no link.
  • After "Present": the toast "<row>: marked attended." with an "Undo" action. The row moves to "Certificate to send" with "Checked in <date time> · Staff". No email goes out while the attendance emails are switched off.
  • The certificate dialog asks "Issue and email a certificate for <person>?" and warns "This cannot be undone."
  • After "Send certificate": the toast "Certificate CERT-WS-... issued. Emailed to the attendee." The row moves to "Done" with "Attended (Staff) · Certificate CERT-WS-... issued". In the inbox: "Certificate of Achievement - TRAINING - Capstone Run - delete after" with the PDF.
  • The export note ends: "This file has no financial data: no amounts, payment details, invoice or receipt numbers. A Finance user can export the full report."
  • After "Download Excel": the toast "Excel download started." and this file:
    • File: naptosa-workshop-report-1-workshop-all-members-no-financial-data-2026-10-08.xlsx

Part 4, the close-out:

  • The summary line for one workshop, in this form: "<date range> · <status> · 1 booking · 1 paid seat · 0 owe R 0,00 · 1 checked in".
  • On "Analytics": the header "Workshop trends" and the "Attendance" card "<a> of <p> paid registrations attended", for the whole province.
  • On "CPTD points": the line "<N> attendees. Select a person to see each workshop." and Sizwe Dube's row opened. The capstone line in his table shows its certificate number and 2 CPTD points.
  • The toast "Registrations closed. The application link is inactive." once for every workshop she closes.
  • Each closed workshop sits under "Closed archive" with a grey "Closed" pill. Its link field reads "Reopen registrations to activate the application link."
  • The "Needs closure" helper reads: "These workshop dates have passed but their registration links are still open. Close registrations to archive them, or edit the dates if the event moved." Once the Term 3 workshop is closed it reads: "No past workshops with open links."
  • The "Closed archive" helper reads: "Closed and cancelled workshops stay here with their registrations, payments, attendance and files."

If she gets stuck: give one hint at a time, and only for the part she is in.

  • Part 1, set up and share:
    • "Before you type, check every line on your planning sheet against what the form asks for."
    • "Think about the picture before you publish: what happens if the wrong one goes up?"
  • Part 2, the money step:
    • "Before you answer, decide whose job this is."
    • "What would Finance need from you to find this payment quickly?"
  • Part 3, the day:
    • "Which of these does the system do by itself, and which only happen when you press a button?"
    • "Before you mark anyone present, what do you need to know?"
  • Part 4, the close-out:
    • "Ask yourself whether the number you are reading is for this workshop or for everyone."
    • "What does he actually want to happen when he says 'delete'?"

If it goes wrong:

Setting up and publishing:

  • A red banner "Workshop could not be created. <reason> Your entries are still here; review them and retry.": she fixes the field it opens and clicks "Create workshop" again. The usual reasons:
    • "Title and description are required."
    • "Use a valid date range where the end is after the start."
    • "Capacity is required. Every workshop needs a maximum number of attendees, or tick Unlimited below."
    • A venue message ending "say where people should go, or set the mode to Venue to be confirmed."
    • A missing points choice shows under the CPTD field instead: "Enter the CPTD points, or tick No CPTD points for this workshop."
  • The short code line says "Already used by another active workshop. Try <X>.": she clicks the "Use <X>" button, or types another code and waits for "Available".
  • After publishing, the toast says "The workshop dates have passed. Review the dates or close registrations before sharing its link." and the workshop sits in "Needs closure": the end time was already past. She clicks "Edit details", fixes Start and End, and clicks "Save changes".
  • "Clipboard access was blocked. Open the workshop, select its Application link field and copy it manually.": she clicks in the "Application link" field (it selects everything) and copies it by hand.

Following the booking and the money:

  • No row in "Owes money": she clicks "Refresh" and checks the "Workshop" select is on the capstone workshop. A row only exists once the booker has clicked "Save details and continue".
  • On your screen, a warning that the receipt email did not send: as Finance, open the row's "..." menu in "Check in" and choose "Resend receipt email". Never mark it paid a second time.

The day:

  • The check-in email is blocked:
    • "This email says the workshop starts today, so it can only be sent on the workshop day (<day>).": the dates are not today. She fixes them in "Edit details".
    • "Set the workshop format (online, in person or hybrid) before emailing check-in details.": she sets "How it runs".
    • "There are no paid attendees to email yet.": Finance has not marked the EFT paid yet, or she needs to click "Refresh".
  • No "Present" button on the row: the row is still in "Owes money", so the booking is unpaid. Go back to the hand-off.
  • Red row text "Could not save. Choose a status again to retry." after "Present": she clicks "Present" again.
  • A warning that the certificate email did not send: she opens the row's "..." menu in "Done" and chooses "Resend certificate email". She does not click "Send certificate" again.
  • Export says "Choose at least one workshop.": nothing is ticked. She ticks the capstone workshop.
  • Export says "Excel export failed. Please retry the download.": she clicks "Download Excel" again.

The close-out:

  • She closes a workshop that is not a TRAINING one: she opens "Closed archive", selects it and clicks "Reopen registrations". She confirms the box "Reopen registrations for "<title>"? The public application link will become active and people can register again." She should see the toast "Workshop link is active. Registrations are open." If that workshop's dates have already passed, the toast is "The workshop dates have passed. Review the dates or close registrations before sharing its link." and it goes back to "Needs closure". The next 00:05 run will close it again. Then she tells you at once.
  • She clicks "Delete workshop" on the capstone: the box says "A payment has been recorded for this workshop, so it cannot be deleted. Payment records are always kept. Cancel the workshop instead. To stop new bookings now, use Close registrations." She clicks "Close" and uses "Close registrations".
  • "Workshop trends could not be loaded. Check your connection and try again.": she clicks "Retry trends".
  • "No CPTD points yet. A workshop counts once the attendee is marked attended and their certificate is issued.": the certificate was not issued. She goes back to "Certificate to send".
  • "Your account cannot see CPTD points. Workshop staff only.": she is on the wrong account. She uses her own workshop staff login.
  • "Nobody matches "...".": she checks the spelling of the surname on the booking, or searches by the test member NAP number instead. The CPTD tab files people under the NAP number they booked with.
  • The CPTD total is more than 2, or the table lists other TRAINING workshops: the CPTD tab groups people by the NAP number they booked with. Certificates from other TRAINING bookings made today with the same test NAP number get added to this row. She reads only the capstone line in the table.
  • "Status update failed." stays on screen: she clicks "Close registrations" again. If it fails twice, she tells you.

Cannot be undone:

  • "TRAINING - Capstone Run - delete after" has a paid booking, so "Delete workshop" refuses it. It stays for good.
  • Sizwe is marked paid for good. That wrote a real invoice and receipt number, so add him to your training receipts list.
  • The EFT instructions, receipt, "Workshop starts today" and certificate emails have gone and cannot be called back.
  • Sizwe's certificate is frozen with the name, points and dates it was issued with.
  • The "Close registrations" clicks can be undone with "Reopen registrations" on any workshop whose dates have not passed. On a workshop whose dates have passed, a reopen is undone at the next 00:05 run.

Before each click that cannot be undone, say it out loud as Coach: "This one cannot be undone." Then click.

If you are running late: Never drop the capstone. Keep the clean-up closes as the very last action, and keep each management answer to one line. If she closes a workshop that is not a TRAINING one, she reopens it straight away and tells you.

Debrief: Straight after the last toast, take one minute: one moment that stood out and one thing to improve. The full debrief runs at 12:55. These are the questions you use there:

  1. For each of the four parts (set up and share, the money step, the day, the close-out): did you do it? We mark each one achieved, partial or missed, with one real moment from the run.
  2. Which one moment stood out for you, and why?
  3. Whose job is "Mark EFT paid", and what do you do instead?
  4. Turn over your start-of-day prediction: which steps did you think were money steps, and what did the run show you? Then write your learning record: what I can now do, where I got stuck, what I still cannot do, when I will practise again.

How did she do?

  • Set up, publish and share the workshop from her own planning sheet.
    • Done well when she typed every value from her planning sheet, saw "Workshop created.", "Workshop link is active. Registrations are open." and "Application link copied." without hints, and sent the link.
    • Partly when she got it published but typed a value that is not on her sheet, needed a hint, or did not get the link to the CEO.
    • Missed when the workshop was not published, or was published with past dates and went straight to "Needs closure".
  • Spot the money step and hand it to Finance.
    • Done well when she found the "Owes money" row, said she cannot mark the EFT paid, gave Finance the name, workshop, amount and EFT reference, and after "Refresh" checked the row in "Check in" with "Paid R 250,00 · EFT".
    • Partly when she handed it over without the reference or the amount, or did not check the result after Finance acted.
    • Missed when she promised the CEO she would mark it paid, went hunting for the button, or skipped the hand-off.
  • Run the day: check-in email, attendance, certificate, export.
    • Done well when she sent the check-in email (result starting "Sent 1"), checked the person was there before "Present", checked the name, saw the certificate toast, said the automatic certificates only go out at 08:00 tomorrow, downloaded the export and named Finance for the money version.
    • Partly when she did the steps but clicked "Present" without asking, did not check the name, or expected something to happen by itself today.
    • Missed when no certificate went out today, or she told the CEO the "Workshop starting soon" email will reach Sizwe.
  • Close out: answer management, say no to delete and refund, close every TRAINING workshop.
    • Done well when she answered from the summary line for this workshop, explained that "Analytics" covers the whole province, read the CPTD total, said no to delete and refund and named the right owner (Finance, with the Provincial CEO's approval), and saw "Registrations closed. The application link is inactive." on the capstone workshop, the Term 3 workshop and every other open TRAINING workshop.
    • Partly when she closed the capstone and Term 3 workshops but left another TRAINING workshop open, quoted "Analytics" as this workshop's numbers, or promised a refund without naming Finance.
    • Missed when she left the capstone link open, closed a workshop that is not a TRAINING one and did not reopen it, or said the system can delete a paid workshop or refund.

12:52 · Three quick questions out loud (3 min, skip if behind)

Goal: She now knows three more things about real workshops. Swapping a poster after people booked is possible, and it is her job. Fixing a misspelt name is not possible for her, so she stops and escalates. Moving a workshop is possible, but the system tells nobody, so she contacts people herself.

Who drives: She answers out loud. You read each question.

What happens:

  1. You tell her the other quiz questions go into her workbook.
  2. You ask the first question and wait.
  3. You ask the second question and wait.
  4. You ask the third question and wait.
  5. After each answer, you give the right answer and the reason in one line.

The questions and the right answers:

  1. A new poster. A workshop was published yesterday and three people have booked. The presenter emails a new poster and asks you to take the old one down. Do it yourself, hand it to Finance, or stop and escalate?
    • Right answer: do it yourself. "Edit details" > "4. Documents & Media" > choose the new picture > "Save changes". The old poster is removed when she saves.
    • Why: files and the poster are the creator's job, and payments do not change that. She should see the toast "Workshop updated." If a warning says the previous poster could not be removed, she checks "Workshop files" and uses "Remove" on the old poster there.
    • Watch for: uploading under "Workshop files" (a second picture there is only a staff reference, not the poster), or closing and making a new workshop (that kills the link three people used).
  2. A misspelt surname. After the workshop, a paid member who attended phones. Her surname is misspelt on her booking and she wants it right on her certificate. Her row is in "Certificate to send", the group for people who attended and still need a certificate. What do you do?
    • Right answer: stop. Do not click "Send certificate". Escalate to the super admin to fix the name, then issue the certificate once the row shows the right spelling.
    • Why: no staff screen can edit a name, and a certificate freezes the name when it is issued. It cannot be undone. The automatic run also sends it at 08:00 the morning after the workshop, so the fix has to happen before then.
    • Watch for: "Edit details" (that edits the workshop, not a person's booking), or asking Finance to cancel and rebook (a paid booking cannot be cancelled: "This booking is paid. Paid bookings need a refund and cannot be cancelled here.").
  3. A postponed workshop. The presenter falls ill and the workshop moves two weeks later. Four people have already paid. What do you do?
    • Right answer: change the dates in "Edit details" and click "Save changes". Then contact the four paid people yourself. Find them in "All Registrations" in "Check in", "Certificate to send" or "Done". Those are the groups that hold paid bookings. Their emails are in your export. Anyone who cannot make the new date goes to Finance as a refund request.
    • Why: the workshop moves with its bookings and receipts unchanged, but there is no automatic "details changed" email on this version of the app. Refunds happen outside the system through Finance, with the CEO's approval.
    • Watch for: expecting the system to email people, or a new workshop with Finance marking the four paid again (that issues a second receipt for money paid once).

Cannot be undone: Nothing in this block.

If you are running late: Drop this block. It is the sixth thing on the drop list. Move all three questions to the next session and say so out loud.

12:55 · Debrief and learning record (15 min)

Goal: She realises that closing a workshop kills the old link and takes it off the website, but keeps every record. She learns how she did on each goal, picks one thing to practise next, and writes her learning record in her own words. She realises how her morning guess about "mine or Finance" compares with what really happened.

Who drives: Both of you. For the proof, she works her own screen and you open the old link and the Events page on the second screen. For the debrief, you coach and she writes.

Before you start:

  • Her checklist sheet with her morning "mine or Finance" marks, still face down.
  • Her start-of-day prediction from 09:04, still face down.
  • The workbook page for her learning record.
  • The old booking link she sent you at the start of the capstone.

What happens:

The proof (about 2 minutes, optional if time is short):

  1. She goes to "Published Links" > "Closed archive" and selects "TRAINING - Capstone Run - delete after". "Closed archive" holds closed workshops with all their records kept.
  2. She reads the "Application link" field out loud.
  3. She points out that "Copy link" is greyed out, that "Booking link QR" is gone, and that "Check-in link & QR" is still there.
  4. You open the old booking link on the second screen.
  5. You open the website Events page on the second screen.
  6. You tell her: "Registration closes by itself at 00:05 the night after a workshop ends. Until then the booking link stays open, so close it yourself as soon as it is over. Never reopen a workshop whose dates have passed, because the next 00:05 run closes it again. If the event moved, edit the dates instead."
  7. She goes to "All Registrations", opens the "Workshop" select and chooses the capstone workshop under "Closed archive".
  8. She finds Sizwe Dube's row in "Done", unchanged. "Done" holds people who attended and got their certificate.

The debrief:

  1. You go through the 12-line completion checklist with her, one line at a time. You mark each line achieved, partial or missed, based on what was actually on screen, with one real moment as the note. These are the 12 lines:
    1. Toast "Workshop created." and the workshop under "Drafts".
    2. Toast "Workshop link is active. Registrations are open."
    3. Toast "Application link copied."
    4. The booking row in "Owes money".
    5. Hand-off said and Finance's toast seen.
    6. Row in "Check in" with "Paid R 250,00 · EFT".
    7. Check-in email result starting "Sent 1".
    8. Toast ending "marked attended."
    9. Toast "Certificate CERT-WS-... issued. Emailed to the attendee."
    10. Toast "Excel download started."
    11. Management answered from the right screens.
    12. Toast "Registrations closed. The application link is inactive." for the capstone workshop, the Term 3 workshop and every other open TRAINING workshop, all in "Closed archive".
  2. You mark each of the four goals achieved, partial or missed, each with one real moment:
    • Set up, publish and share: done well if she typed every value from her planning sheet, got all three toasts without hints and sent the link.
    • The money step: done well if she gave Finance the name, workshop, amount and EFT reference and checked "Paid R 250,00 · EFT" after "Refresh".
    • The day: done well if she sent the check-in email, checked he was there before "Present", checked the name before the certificate, and named Finance for the money export.
    • The close-out: done well if she answered from the right screens, said no to delete and refund with Finance named, and closed every TRAINING workshop.
  3. You ask: "Which one moment stood out for you, and why?"
  4. You name three things she did well, as actions. Say what she did, not "good communication".
  5. You ask: "Whose job is "Mark EFT paid", and what do you do instead?"
  6. You agree on ONE concrete next practice with her. For example:
    • run the hand-off again with a member who pushes back, or
    • redo the close-out on a workshop with past dates.
  7. She turns over her start-of-day prediction from 09:04.
  8. She compares which steps she thought were hers or Finance's with what actually happened.
  9. She writes her learning record in her own words, on the workbook page:
    • What I can now do:
    • Where I got stuck:
    • What I still cannot do:
    • When I will practise again: a date
  10. You ask the anchor question again: "Is this mine to do, or do I recognise it and hand it to Finance?"
  11. She gives one example of each from the run.
  12. You name anything that was skipped or only partly done, for example a TRAINING workshop left open, or the proof if you dropped it. You say when each one will be covered. No summary slide.

She should see:

  • The link field reads "Reopen registrations to activate the application link." "Copy link" is greyed out, there is no "Booking link QR" button, and "Check-in link & QR" is still there.
  • On your screen, the old booking link shows "This workshop can't be opened" and "This workshop is not available for payment."
  • The Events page no longer lists the capstone workshop or any other TRAINING workshop she closed.
  • In "All Registrations": Sizwe Dube's row in "Done" with "Attended (Staff) · Certificate CERT-WS-... issued".
  • The checklist with all 12 lines marked achieved, partial or missed.
  • Her learning record on the workbook page, with a practice date.
  • A written list of anything skipped or partly done, with a follow-up time.

If it goes wrong:

  • The Events page still lists a TRAINING workshop: it was not closed and its end time has not passed yet. She selects it under "Current & upcoming" or "Needs closure" and clicks "Close registrations" once.
  • The old link still opens the booking form: the capstone workshop was not closed. She checks "Closed archive". If it is not there, she closes it now.
  • She is frustrated after a missed step: tell her it is normal ("this part is tricky, most people need two passes") and skip the closing question.
  • She asks "did I pass?": answer from the checklist marks, not from a general feeling.
  • Time runs out, or the capstone ran over: take the time out of this debrief, never out of the learning record. Drop the proof first, then the line-by-line marks, but keep the four goal marks. If the three questions at 12:52 were skipped, move them to the next session and say so out loud.

Cannot be undone: Nothing in this block.

If you are running late: Drop the proof first, then the line-by-line marks (keep the four goal marks). Never cut the learning record. After 13:10, you close any TRAINING workshop she missed, and never close one twice.

After the session

Do these the same evening. The times in brackets are a guide.

  1. (10 min, as Finance) Cancel every unpaid TRAINING booking. On your super-admin screen, go to "All Registrations" > "Owes money" and click "Cancel booking" on each one. "Owes money" holds bookings that are not paid yet.
    • Reason: Other
    • Reason text: Training - delete after

Each one sends a "Booking cancelled" email to the address on that booking: her own email for the bookings she made, loxly@lbtec.co.za for the ones you made.

  1. (5 min) Click "Close registrations" on every TRAINING workshop still open. The Events page drops a workshop by itself after its end time and the nightly run closes it at 00:05 that night, but a workshop dated in the future keeps taking bookings until you close it:
    • "TRAINING - Classroom Discipline - delete after" (the one she built in the morning)
    • "TRAINING - Chasing the money - delete after"
    • "TRAINING - Classroom Management Essentials - delete after"
    • "TRAINING - Term 3 Curriculum Planning - delete after" (if you made it)
    • "TRAINING - Capstone Run - delete after"
  2. Check that every one of them sits in "Closed archive".
  3. (2 min) Click "Close check-in" on any workshop where check-in was opened. Check-in is the page people use at the door to sign themselves in.
  4. (2 min) Open the public Events page and check that no TRAINING workshop is listed.
  5. (5 min) Send Finance the training receipts list. For each one give:
    • Payer name: for the capstone, Sizwe Dube
    • Workshop: the full title
    • Invoice and receipt numbers: the INV and RCT numbers

Every training "Mark EFT paid" is a real payment row. It counts in "Revenue collected" on "Analytics" and in the Finance Excel totals. So Finance must mark these as training, with no bank receipt, in the monthly reconciliation.

  1. (3 min, as Finance) Switch off the training coupon. Open the workshop's coupon list and click "Switch off" on its row, then "Switch off" in the box. It is refused while an unpaid booking still holds the code, so do it after step 1.
  2. (3 min) Give the super admin the clean-up list:
    • any stray drafts
    • any certificate name fixes
    • any cancelled TRAINING bookings you want gone. Finance removes them in "All Registrations" > "Cancelled" > "Remove". Only bookings with no payment history show "Remove". The rest say "Kept for the money record: this booking has a payment history."
  3. (2 min) Ask the super admin to review the CRM contacts the training bookings created. Each individual Western Cape TRAINING booking created or filled in a CRM contact, with the source "Workshop". These test contacts need to be reviewed and removed.
  4. Before anything is purged, check what is in each row and what depends on it. Never purge on a label alone.
  5. (2 min) Go to Settings > Roles and check her account still has Workshop creator ON and Finance OFF.
  6. (5 min) File her learning record and your scoring sheet together.
  7. Write down every block you dropped or only partly ran, and the date it will be covered.
  8. (Follow-up, in 2 to 3 days) Ask her to write the 10 steps of a workshop's life and the "mine or Finance" list from memory, without looking.
  9. Check her list against the where-to-look sheet.
  10. (Follow-up, at her own pace) Give her one short lesson per part from the practice cards you did not run today, in this order. Each one ends with "come back in a few days and recall it without looking."
    1. "Put the Zoom link on the poster": switching a workshop to Online or Hybrid and seeing where the joining link really goes. Do this one first. It is the one real gap in what she has learned.
    2. The brief changes before publishing: edit the draft and re-check the short code.
    3. Which account do people pay into, and when are they reminded (read-only for a creator).
    4. The poster and agenda arrive late.
    5. "Can we test the door QR today?"
    6. A non-member looks at the card option, gives up, then comes back and pays by EFT.
    7. Coupon codes: a fixed-price coupon and a free-place coupon.
    8. The workshop fills while an EFT booking is still unpaid.
    9. The member portal: a booking waiting for payment, "Pay now", and the wrong-email empty page.
    10. "It charged me the non-member price."
    11. "I cannot find the bank details" and "my cell number is rejected."
    12. "Send me the banking details again."
    13. "I want to pay by card now."
    14. "The school will pay for me."
    15. "I typed my email wrong so I never got the receipt."
    16. The member who gets a reminder and pays.
    17. "Why did he get a reminder and she didn't?"
    18. A member cannot attend: cancelling an unpaid booking is a Finance hand-off.
    19. Booked twice: finding the duplicate and cancelling the right one.
    20. Wrong email and misspelt surname on a booking.
    21. "My card was declined."
    22. The workshop was last week and the link is still open.
    23. Another province asks her to help: what she can and cannot see.
    24. Four things the system cannot do yet: refund, moving a paid booking, part payment, cancelling the whole workshop.
    25. The day before: the door QR, opening check-in early, and the email that cannot go out yet.
    26. Morning of the workshop: send "Workshop starts today", then handle "I never got it".
    27. Self check-in at the door for a member who booked with a NAP number.
    28. A member receives her certificate: email, PDF, the member portal, and the empty-portal trap.
    29. Finance wants the paid list, and her export has no money in it.
    30. The school-place teacher on the day.
    31. "I was there!": the absent member and fixing attendance after the day.
  11. (Her next real workshop) She plans it on her own planning sheet, then creates and runs it. You sit beside her as Finance for the money steps.
  12. Book a second session for anything dropped today.
  13. (A note for you) Fix the role legend at the front of the training pack before anyone else uses it. "Delete workshop" is there for Workshop creators and super admins, but it only works while nobody has paid. Reminder rules are changed by the super admin, case admin, admin, supervisor or Finance, not by the Workshop creator tick.

Resetting between scenes

There is no reset button on production. You plan forward instead. Keep these in mind for every run of this training.

  1. Use the right email for every booking:
    • Bookings she makes: her own email, <Trainee work email> or <Trainee personal email>, so she receives and sees every email of her own bookings.
    • Bookings you make: loxly@lbtec.co.za.
    • A deliberately wrong address: wrong.address@example.com.
  2. Reusing one email on the same workshop is expected. The "Possible existing registration" panel appears with the reason "same email, same telephone". She clicks "Save as additional entry". A second PAID booking for the same person is still refused, so mark only one booking per person paid on each workshop.
  3. Leave an unpaid booking you no longer need until the evening clean-up.
  4. If an unpaid booking gets in the way now, you (as Finance, on the super-admin screen) cancel it. Go to "All Registrations" > "Owes money" and click "Cancel booking". "Owes money" holds bookings that are not paid yet. Fill in:
    • Reason: Other
    • Reason text: Training - delete after

This sends a "Booking cancelled" email to the address on that booking. The trainee never does this.

  1. If a booking is already in the "Cancelled" group and has no payment history, Finance removes it in "All Registrations" > "Cancelled" > "Remove". Fill in:
    • Reason: Booked by mistake

Then click "Remove booking". A cancelled booking with a payment history has no "Remove" and says "Kept for the money record: this booking has a payment history." "Remove" is also still in "Published Links" > select the workshop > "Attendee ledger". The Attendee ledger is the full list of bookings for one workshop.

  1. "Remind" is Finance's button for sending an EFT payment reminder by hand. It works once per person per South African day. Use a different person for every "Remind". A second one on the same person that day is refused.
  2. To fix a wrong attendance mark, click "Undo" on the toast.
  3. If the toast has gone, fix attendance in "Published Links" > select the workshop > "Attendee ledger" > the "Attendance" select. While the attendance emails are switched off, no email goes out either way.
  4. Click "Close check-in" at the end of every check-in scene so the page is not left open.
  5. Click "Close registrations" when a workshop's part of the day is over. That is her "TRAINING - Classroom Discipline - delete after" at the end of the changes and problems part, and "TRAINING - Capstone Run - delete after" at the end of the capstone.
  6. If you close the wrong one, open "Closed archive" > that workshop > "Reopen registrations" and confirm. The toast reads "Workshop link is active. Registrations are open."
  7. Know what cannot be undone:
    • A booking marked paid: the payment row, its invoice and receipt number and its receipt email stay forever, and the amount counts in "Revenue collected" on "Analytics". Add it to the training receipts list straight away.
    • A sent email (EFT instructions, reminder, receipt, cancellation, check-in, certificate): it has gone and cannot be called back.
    • An issued certificate: it cannot be withdrawn. The name, points, SACE number and dates are frozen, and the person now shows in "CPTD points".
    • A removed file: a file can be taken off with "Remove", but the removal cannot be undone.
    • A coupon's code, kind and price: they cannot be changed. Finance can only change how many people may use it, or switch it off and on again.
    • A workshop with any payment on it: it cannot be deleted, only closed. A workshop with no payment can be deleted with "Delete workshop", and that cannot be undone either.
    • A removed cancelled booking: it is gone for good.
  8. Before each of those clicks, say it out loud as Coach: "This one cannot be undone." Then click.

If something breaks

  • The staff app will not load, or she cannot sign in: reload, check the Wi-Fi, try the other browser. Never sign her in with your account. If it is still down after 5 minutes, run the paper version: the where-to-look sheet walk-through, the sort-the-actions cards and the quiz. Move the hands-on blocks to a second session, and name what you moved at the close.
  • Money buttons ("Mark EFT paid", "Remind", "Cancel booking") show on HER screen: stop. Her Finance tick is on. Do not click anything. Fix it in Settings > Roles on your super-admin screen, have her reload, and check the buttons are gone before you carry on.
  • She pressed Enter in a single-line field and the form saved too early: this is normal and catches most people. Find the workshop under "Drafts", open "Edit details", finish the fields and click "Save changes". The extra draft stays in "Drafts". Add its title to your clean-up list.
  • The short code line says "Already used by another active workshop": the short code is the code of up to 6 letters or digits that goes into the EFT payment reference, and each active workshop needs its own. She clicks the "Use <X>" button the system offers, or types another code and waits for "Available" before saving.
  • "Copy link" or "Open link" does nothing useful: the workshop is still a draft, or its date has passed. Publish it first, or check the date.
  • A new booking or coupon does not show on the staff side: click "Refresh" for bookings. Coupons do not update with "Refresh". Click away and back, or reload the page.
  • The 09:00 automatic EFT reminder did not reach the bookings on "TRAINING - Chasing the money - delete after" (check this at the 10:25 break): run the "I paid on Monday, why am I still being chased?" phone call anyway, and change the caller's line to "I got your payment email". If you need a reminder in the inbox, click "Remind" yourself as Finance on that person, once only, and say it as Narrator.
  • "Email check-in details to attendees" refuses to send: it only works on the workshop's own day. Use a workshop dated 2026-10-08: "TRAINING - Classroom Management Essentials - delete after" or "TRAINING - Capstone Run - delete after".
  • The phone shows that check-in is not open: open "Check-in link & QR" and click "Open check-in now" > "Yes, open check-in". Click "Close check-in" when the scene ends.
  • "Present" will not go on for someone: that person has not paid. Attendance is only for paid bookings. This is the hand-off moment: she tells Finance, she does not work around it.
  • A wrong "Absent" and the "Undo" toast has gone: "Published Links" > select the workshop > "Attendee ledger" > the "Attendance" select for that row. The Attendee ledger is the full list of bookings for one workshop.
  • A certificate went out with the wrong name: it cannot be withdrawn or edited in the app. Teach her the honest answer: the name is frozen when the certificate is issued, and a correction is a data fix by the system administrator outside the app. Add it to your clean-up list. Do not promise a date.
  • "Remove" does not show for a cancelled booking: the booking has a payment history. The row says "Kept for the money record: this booking has a payment history." That is correct. Skip the step and say in one sentence that money records are never removed.
  • The "Possible existing registration" panel appears: that email was already used on that workshop. The reason reads "same email, same telephone". Click "Save as additional entry". If a second paid booking for the same person is refused, that person already has a paid place on that workshop. Book the next paid one as a different training person.
  • "Remind" says the person already had a reminder today: this is expected. It works once per person per South African day. Use a different training person.
  • The wrong workshop was closed (for example the capstone, or "TRAINING - Classroom Management Essentials - delete after" before its day is over): "Closed archive" > that workshop > "Reopen registrations" and confirm. Check for the toast "Workshop link is active. Registrations are open."
  • Someone clicks save on Settings > "Payment details": a change alert email has already gone to real staff. Tell the Provincial CEO straight away that it was a training slip, and check the EFT details still match what real payers should see.
  • A real person (not one of the training bookings) books a published TRAINING workshop from the website: do not mark it paid. Close registrations on that workshop now. Contact the person by hand from the office mailbox to explain. Then, as Finance, cancel the unpaid booking with a clear reason.
  • She reaches for your super-admin screen: as Coach, stop her kindly. Say: "That screen is real Finance. One click there cannot be undone. Tell me what you want done and I will do it as Finance."
  • You are running more than 10 minutes late: drop blocks in this order. Replace each one with its one-line summary, and name it at the close with when it will be covered.
    1. The member who typed her email wrong, gets a reminder at the corrected address, then cancels.
    2. The coupon hands-on.
    3. The "Stop reminding me" phone call from the member who is not coming.
    4. The member booking hands-on.
    5. The export hands-on.
    6. The three quick questions at 12:52.

Scoring sheet

Tick each line only when you saw it happen.

Part 1: Orientation, the map and the rules

  • [ ] Names the 10 steps of a workshop's life in order: pass when she says or writes create, publish, share, follow bookings, chase payment, handle changes, run the day, attendance, certificates, exports, in that order, and her 09:04 sheet is in the envelope.
  • [ ] Opens the six tabs and says what each is for: pass when she clicks "Create Workshop", "Published Links", "All Registrations", "Analytics", "Reminders" and "CPTD points" on her own account and gives one correct sentence for each.
  • [ ] Sorts actions into "mine" and "Finance": pass when "Mark EFT paid", "Create coupon", "Cancel booking", "Remove", "Remind", "Correct payer email" and the money export land on Finance, and "Publish workshop", "Close registrations", "Present", "Send certificate" and the no-money export land on hers.
  • [ ] Says who counts as Finance: pass when she names the Finance tick, the auditor role and a super admin, says a case admin, admin or supervisor is not Finance unless a super admin ticked it, and says that in the Western Cape today Finance means the super admins only, because nobody has the Finance tick and there is no auditor. She says this in words; there is no auditor row to point at.
  • [ ] States the four practice rules and why: pass when she says TRAINING workshop only, her own work or personal email for every booking she makes, EFT only and never a real card, and that anything marked paid, emailed or certified cannot be undone.

Part 2: Set up and publish

  • [ ] Creates a workshop from her own planning sheet: pass when she copies every value from her planning sheet, fills in the four sections, clicks "Create workshop", sees the toast "Workshop created." and the workshop sits under "Drafts".
  • [ ] Gets a short code nobody else is using: pass when she waits for "Available" under "Short code" (or clicks the "Use <X>" button) before saving, and the short code shows under the title.
  • [ ] Checks the preview before publishing: pass when she opens "Preview", reads the member view and the "Certificate preview", and closes it.
  • [ ] Publishes and shares: pass when she clicks "Publish workshop", sees the toast "Workshop link is active. Registrations are open.", the workshop is in "Current & upcoming", she uses "Copy link", "Open link" and "Booking link QR", and she finds it on the Events page.
  • [ ] Adds the poster and agenda and reads the header numbers: pass when the poster is saved through "Edit details", the agenda is uploaded through "Workshop files", and she reads "seats paid", "bookings incomplete", "Capacity" and "Price" out loud correctly.
  • [ ] Names the four limits of this part of the app: pass when, without looking, she says "Delete workshop" only works while nobody has paid (otherwise close registrations), removing a file with "Remove" cannot be undone, the poster shows on the booking page only and never on the Events page, and a booking link stays open after the workshop ends until she clicks "Close registrations" or the nightly run closes it at 00:05.

Part 3: Booking and paying, from the attendee's chair

  • [ ] Books as a non-member by EFT: pass when the pending screen with the training bank details shows, the "Workshop EFT payment instructions" email is in her own inbox, and after "Refresh" she shows the "bookings incomplete" count up by one.
  • [ ] Books as a member at the member rate: pass when she uses the "NAP number (preferred) or SA ID number" field with the test value, "View details" shows the member rate by EFT, and she says the number is not looked up.
  • [ ] Applies a Finance coupon: pass when the lower "Amount due" (or "Confirm free booking") shows on the payment step, and she says creating coupons is Finance's job.
  • [ ] Books for a school and hands payment to Finance: pass when she says the hand-off sentence, the school's receipt email shows the place code after Finance clicks "Mark EFT paid", and one teacher claims a place with that code.
  • [ ] Finds each booking in the right group: pass when she finds the unpaid ones in "Owes money" and the paid ones in "Check in", and reads the rate, method, reference and email status in "View details".

Part 4: Chasing the money

  • [ ] Finds a person and reads their history: pass when she finds a booking by name, email or EFT reference and reads its group, badges and reminder history in "View details".
  • [ ] Reads the reminder rules correctly: pass when, in "Reminders" > "Reminder settings", she says which rules send email, and says "Workshop starting soon" is switched off until a super admin turns it on, so the "Workshop starts today" email is used instead.
  • [ ] Handles "I paid on Monday": pass when she does not look for a paid button, gives Finance the name, workshop, amount and EFT reference, and then shows the row in "Check in" and the receipt in the inbox.
  • [ ] Explains why the reminders keep coming: pass when she tells the member the reminders stop only when the unpaid booking is cancelled, and that "Cancel booking" is Finance's.
  • [ ] Uses her own receipt tools and names Finance's: pass when she uses "Resend receipt email" or "Download receipt (PDF)", and names "Mark EFT paid", "Remind", "Send an EFT payment reminder now", "Cancel booking" and "Correct payer email" as Finance only.

Part 5: Changes and problems

  • [ ] Edits after people have booked: pass when she changes the venue, date or price with "Edit details" > "Save changes" and shows in "View details" that an existing booking keeps the amount it was booked at.
  • [ ] Decides what to tell people about a change: pass when she says no automatic email goes out, collects the emails with the CSV export, and gives a sensible plan for the paid and the unpaid people.
  • [ ] Spots and hands off cancel, correct email and remove: pass when, for each request, she says the hand-off sentence and does not try it herself, and it is done only after Finance acts.
  • [ ] Closes registrations: pass when she clicks "Close registrations", sees the toast "Registrations closed. The application link is inactive." and finds the workshop in "Closed archive" with its records.
  • [ ] Names the four things the system cannot do: pass when she names refunds, cancelling a paid booking, part payment, and cancelling the whole workshop with notice, and gives the manual way round each one (refunds go to Finance or the Provincial CEO, paid people are contacted by hand, registrations are closed).

Part 6: On the day and after

  • [ ] Opens and closes check-in early: pass when she opens "Check-in link & QR", uses "Open check-in now" > "Yes, open check-in", the phone shows the check-in page, and she clicks "Close check-in" afterwards.
  • [ ] Sends the day-of email: pass when, on the workshop dated today, she reads the preview count, sends "Email check-in details to attendees", and the "Workshop starts today" email is in the inbox.
  • [ ] Keeps the register: pass when she marks "Present" one and two at a time, uses "Undo", marks one "Mark absent", the rows move to "Certificate to send", and she refuses to mark the unpaid person and hands them to Finance.
  • [ ] Sends certificates: pass when she uses "Send certificate" and "Send all N", the certificate email and PDF are in the inbox, the rows move to "Done", she says the name, points, SACE number and dates are frozen, and she says certificates also go out by themselves at 08:00 the morning after the workshop to everyone who paid and was marked present.
  • [ ] Exports and reads CPTD points: pass when she runs "Export Excel", shows it has no money columns, reads one person's points in "CPTD points", and names who runs the money export.

Part 7: Capstone

  • [ ] Runs one workshop end to end without hints: pass when every completion checklist line is ticked from what was on screen, with no hint given and no Coach step-in.
  • [ ] Stops at every money step: pass when, at "Mark EFT paid", she says the hand-off with the name, workshop, amount and EFT reference and waits, and she also names Finance for the money export and any refund.
  • [ ] Answers management and closes: pass when she answers from the "All Registrations" summary line, "Analytics" and "CPTD points", says which number is this workshop and which is the province, clicks "Close registrations" and shows the workshop in "Closed archive".
  • [ ] Writes her learning record: pass when the four learning record lines are written in her own words and she compares them out loud with her 09:04 prediction.
